## Daily Activity

Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! This week, we’re looking at a [**Magic Online**](https://web.archive.org/web/20140412005615/http://www.wizards.com/Magic/Digital/MagicOnline.aspx?x=mtg/digital/magiconline/whatshappening) *Avacyn Restored* Draft.
